PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [178] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，一种即插即用的线性复杂度注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵与输入相关残差分量替代标准自注意力中的查询-键投影，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时显著提升时间序列预测的推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力机制在时间序列预测中具有关于回溯长度的二次时间和内存复杂度，这限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量预测系统中的应用。作者通过分析发现时间序列中的自注意力图在不同时间戳上存在冗余模式，这为设计更高效的注意力机制提供了机会。

Method: 提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，将注意力分数表示为共享可学习矩阵（捕获通用注意力模式）与输入相关残差分量（捕获输入相关变化）之和，完全避免标准注意力中的查询和键投影，从而实现关于回溯长度的线性时间复杂度和线性得分矩阵内存复杂度。

Result: 在覆盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动和气候记录等领域的9个真实数据集上，集成SGA的多个预测骨干网络在保持与最先进注意力机制相当预测性能的同时，显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA作为一种轻量级、即插即用的注意力替代方案，能够有效解决标准自注意力的计算瓶颈，为时间序列预测的部署场景提供了高效且性能稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [179] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL，一种通过强化学习让LLM学会将复杂问题分解为可独立求解的子模块，并通过组合子模块实现指数级候选解空间扩展的方法，相比传统采样和RL方法，大幅降低GPU计算成本（约50倍），并在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（重复采样和强化学习）在基策略生成正确答案概率接近零时均会失效，因为搜索空间过大导致采样或梯度信号都无法克服。作者希望通过将问题分解为更小的可独立求解子函数，并重组实现，从而降低任务难度。

Method: 提出DecompRL算法，让LLM显式学习分解并实现层次化代码结构。将问题分解为n个模块，每个模块有k种实现，组合后产生k^n个候选解，通过将计算瓶颈从GPU推理转移到CPU评估，实现约50倍GPU token成本降低。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上，使用Qwen 2.5 7B和Code World Model 32B，DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 token时优于标准RL和多样性优化RL基线，能够解决标准生成方法无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 通过分解-重组策略，可以有效克服基策略的零概率困境，在保持解多样性的同时大幅降低推理成本，为LLM解决复杂问题提供了一种新的高效范式。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [180] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuFS框架，利用神经元激活模式替代传统输出级信号进行主动少样本学习样本选择，通过双重标准（多样性+难例识别）提升LLM在专业领域的适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFSL方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵、外部嵌入语义相似度）进行样本选择，忽略了模型内部动态（如神经元激活模式）所揭示的知识缺口，导致样本选择不够精准。

Method: NeuFS框架：1) 直接使用神经元激活模式表示样本；2) 双重选择策略——基于神经元模式的多样性确保样本覆盖范围，通过量化神经元共识度优先识别LLM容易产生幻觉的信息性/挑战性样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线；消融实验证明内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更有效且更 principled 的选择信号。

Conclusion: 从输出级信号转向模型内部动态（神经元激活）进行主动少样本学习样本选择是更优的范式，NeuFS有效降低了标注成本并提升了LLM在专业领域的性能。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [181] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架QFedAgent，用于多智能体活动识别，通过变分量子电路融合模块大幅减少参数（72个量子参数 vs 33K经典参数），在OPPORTUNITY数据集上达到97.7%的测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在隐私保护的机器人感知应用中具有潜力，但多智能体系统产生异构非独立同分布的多模态传感器数据会降低传统联邦学习算法性能，且经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本。

Method: 提出QFedAgent框架，集成变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，基于主体非独立同分布划分下达到97.7%的平均测试准确率，参数效率显著优于经典多层感知机融合（72 vs 33K参数，约10倍参数缩减）。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合在联邦学习多模态活动识别任务中与传统联邦基线方法相比具有竞争力，验证了量子-经典混合方法在资源受限场景下的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [182] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exformer（极端自适应Transformer），通过引入包含Local、Stride和Extreme三种稀疏注意力的极端自适应注意力机制，显式建模正常事件与极端事件之间的时序依赖关系，在四个真实水文径流数据集上实现了优于现有方法的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中罕见的极端事件（如洪水峰值）对预测至关重要，但现有Transformer模型对所有时间点均匀处理，难以充分捕捉极端模式，导致对极端事件的预测能力不足。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏组件：Local（捕获短时序依赖）、Stride（捕获周期性依赖）和Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于所有最先进的基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力机制能够提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [183] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。基于理论发现提出了MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论结论和MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但现有研究缺乏对不同D-SSL框架应对该挑战的理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 对D-SSL框架在non-IID设置下进行严格的理论鲁棒性分析；2) 提出MAR损失函数——MIM目标与局部到全局对齐正则化的改进。

Result: 理论证明：MIM预训练比CL对异构数据更鲁棒；D-SSL鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提高，即FL不弱于DecL。实验验证了理论见解及MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 该理论为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，MAR损失作为理论应用的一个实例被验证有效。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [184] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，利用神经元激活信号指导无标注自蒸馏中的训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略蒸馏训练模型，在提升领域内任务性能的同时保持跨域泛化并缓解校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标注自进化方法（如SFT/GRPO变体、基于奖励的在线策略RL）存在域外性能退化或校准误差膨胀问题，而专家标注成本高昂，需一种无需真实标签、不依赖外部交互反馈的自监督方法。

Method: 利用内部神经元激活值指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建；通过模型自身输出构建教师分布，采用在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）进行训练，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准上，Neuron-OPSD提升了领域内任务性能，保持了跨域泛化能力，并缓解了校准误差膨胀问题，优于现有无标注基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂的场景，与依赖已标注轨迹的离线RL方法有本质区别，为无标注自蒸馏提供了有效的数据中心化方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型上的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其在部分力监督条件下提升更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP领域在架构和数据集上投入大量精力，但优化器选择长期被忽视，默认使用Adam及其变体，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型上，实现并系统比较了Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon三类矩阵结构优化器与Adam的性能差异。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；Muon仅带来部分提升；在部分力监督训练下性能提升尤为突出。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响重大的设计维度，矩阵结构优化器有潜力替代Adam成为新的默认选择。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [186] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD提出了一种选择性采纳教师引导的自蒸馏框架，通过反向KL重心目标平衡教师与学生分布，有效缓解特权信息泄漏并保留探索能力，在科学推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)方法中，教师基于特权信息(如正确答案)的密集token级监督会导致：(1) 对域内模式过拟合，抑制探索，损害跨域泛化；(2) 更根本的**特权信息泄漏**问题——学生编码了测试时不存在的答案相关捷径。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是**选择性采纳教师指导**：不拟合完整教师分布，而是引导学生朝向**反向KL重心目标**——教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合。通过衡量分布差异，在每个token位置自适应控制混合比例。理论上证明了该方法可实现泄漏衰减和探索保留。

Result: 在SciKnowEval数据集（覆盖四个科学领域）上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO；在训练中保持更高熵，并在OOD的GPQA基准上表现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过自适应选择性蒸馏，在保留学生自身推理能力的同时有效吸收教师知识，为token级自蒸馏中的特权信息泄漏和探索抑制问题提供了理论保证与实用解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模糊函数编程范式，通过PAW方法将自然语言规范编译为轻量级神经工件，在本地高效执行。4B编译器在FuzzyBench上训练，生成参数高效适配器供冻结的0.6B解释器使用，性能媲美32B模型直接提示，内存仅需约1/50，推理速度达30 tokens/s（MacBook M3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常编程任务（日志告警、JSON修复、搜索结果排序等）难以通过规则实现，日益依赖大模型API，导致本地性、可复现性和成本问题。旨在将自然语言规范编译为紧凑、本地可执行的神经工件，摆脱API依赖。

Method: 提出Program-as-Weights (PAW)方法：一个4B编译器（在10M样本数据集FuzzyBench上训练）将自然语言函数规范编译为参数高效适配器（LoRA等），供一个冻结的轻量级0.6B解释器（Qwen3）执行。编译器一次性生成可复用工件，后续调用无需大模型推理。

Result: 0.6B Qwen3解释器执行PAW程序，性能匹配直接提示Qwen3-32B；推理内存仅约1/50；在MacBook M3上达到30 tokens/s。

Conclusion: PAW将基础模型从逐输入问题求解器重新定位为工具构建器：一次函数定义调用产生可复用小工件，后续函数应用调用廉价且可离线执行，兼顾性能、效率和本地性。

Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: X-VAE通过用预训练自编码器的隐变量统计量替代标准正态先验，并引入潜在缩放因子控制生成方差，在保持重建质量的同时提升了隐空间表征与数据分布的匹配度，实现了生成样本多样性与保真度的可调节平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE假设各向同性高斯先验，这一假设对于复杂数据集往往无法匹配真实隐空间分布，导致重建精度下降、样本质量降低以及隐空间表达能力受限。

Method: 提出X-VAE框架：(1) 用预训练自编码器隐编码的均值和标准差参数化数据自适应高斯先验；(2) 引入潜在缩放因子，在生成时显式控制采样方差，从而平衡样本多样性与保真度。

Result: 实验表明X-VAE在保持重建质量的前提下，使隐表征更好地对齐经验数据分布，提升了可控性并生成更真实的样本。

Conclusion: X-VAE适用于工业与工程设计等需满足严格结构/功能约束同时允许有意义设计探索的场景，为平衡生成多样性和保真度提供了简洁机制。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [189] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出LF-IBIS算法，一种无需显式似然函数的贝叶斯强化学习方法，通过结合近似贝叶斯计算与迭代批量重要性采样，实现在环境动态不可知情况下的完整贝叶斯推断，并在临床试验响应自适应随机化等场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯强化学习方法需要显式似然函数，但在许多实际应用中似然函数难以获取或难以处理，这限制了BRL的应用范围。

Method: 将近似贝叶斯计算(ABC)与迭代批量重要性采样(IBIS)相结合，提出LF-IBIS算法，实现在线信念更新，无需显式似然函数即可对环境参数和最优策略进行近似后验推断。

Result: 在临床试验响应自适应随机化的仿真研究中，该方法能够得到可验证的后验分布；在无闭式后验的场景中也能进行在线策略更新。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS有效地将BRL扩展到似然不可知的场景，为探索-利用权衡提供了贝叶斯框架下的不确定性量化手段。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [190] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了√n-超额风险界和估计聚类中心的一致性，在MCAR机制下推导了√n-收敛速率和渐近正态性，并给出了估计中心收敛到真实中心的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-means无法直接处理不完整数据，现有方法主要关注实际聚类精度提升，但缺乏渐近意义上的理论保证。本文旨在为缺失数据下的k-means聚类提供严格的统计理论基础。

Method: 在一般缺失机制下建立了√n-超额风险界和聚类中心估计的一致性；在MCAR机制下推导了√n-收敛速率和渐近正态性；给出了估计聚类中心收敛到真实聚类中心的充分条件（涉及缺失概率与真实簇间分离度）。

Result: 证明了在MCAR机制下，实现√n-速率和收敛到真实聚类中心都要求k个真实中心在每一维上不同，揭示了高维应用中的显著挑战。数值模拟支持了理论分析结果。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据k-means聚类提供了完整的渐近理论保证，明确了其收敛到真实中心所需的条件，指出了高维场景下的局限性。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [191] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的方法，结合Ghost变量、Shapley值和加法重要性度量，引入自相关和偏自相关函数作为滞后重要性值，并设计了用一步预测替代缺失特征的新方法，用于评估时间序列预测模型中各滞后的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型缺乏对滞后特征重要性的系统性评估方法，尤其是模型无关的、可解释的滞后相关性度量框架。

Method: 基于Ghost变量和Shapley值的框架，结合加法重要性度量，定义自相关函数和偏自相关函数作为滞后重要性值；并提出用模型的一步预测替代缺失特征参与联盟计算的新方法。

Result: 在SARMA和RNN等多种模型上的仿真与真实数据实验中，所提出的相关性度量在几乎所有情况下成功捕捉了预期的滞后结构。

Conclusion: 该框架有效且适用于时间序列场景，为机器学习预测模型提供了可解释的滞后重要性分析工具。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [192] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题替换为连续变分替代模型，在加权Sobolev空间中对参数密度定义λ-凸泛函，证明该变分问题全局适定、稳定且具有几乎C³正则性。通过求解单一线性系统即可获得最优参数密度，并建立显式泛化误差控制（1/α速率）和有限宽度网络以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络虽有效但其优化原理缺乏理论支撑，面临非凸景观和随机启发式方法的困境，亟需建立更严谨的理论框架。

Method: 将离散训练问题转化为连续变分模型，在加权Sobolev空间中定义λ-凸泛函，利用椭圆正则性和凸分析替代Wasserstein/Mean-Field方法，通过求解线性系统直接获得最优参数密度。

Result: 证明了变分问题的全局适定性、稳定性和几乎C³正则性；建立了1/α速率的泛化误差控制；证明了有限宽度网络以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该框架桥接了神经正切核(NTK)与特征学习范式之间的鸿沟，为理解过度参数化提供了基于变分演算的理论基础。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [193] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种决策理论框架，用于不确定性感知的反事实决策，定义了"策略耦合覆盖"作为不确定性与行动之间的最优接口，并开发了PC-RACP两阶段方法，在有限样本下保证覆盖的同时提升效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）仅提供统计有效性（覆盖保证），但统计有效性本身并不能直接指导最优决策。特别是在反事实场景中，结果取决于所采取的行动，不确定性无法独立于决策规则来指定，存在理论与实际决策之间的缺口。

Method: 1. 提出"策略耦合覆盖"（policy-coupled coverage）概念；2. 证明该覆盖下的最大-最小决策规则在分布模糊下是最小最大最优的；3. 证明优化策略耦合覆盖等价于通用覆盖公式和直接风险厌恶优化；4. 提出两阶段程序PC-RACP（Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction），通过有限样本近似最优预测集。

Result: 模拟实验和真实邮件营销实验表明，PC-RACP在保持有效覆盖的同时，比现有方法带来更高的效用；忽略反事实决策结构会导致效力和效用的次优性。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性与决策的最优接口，PC-RACP方法能够在有限样本下实现有效的覆盖保证和更高的决策效用。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [194] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的加性深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符（化学分支）与分子图拓扑（结构分支）分离建模，仅在预测阶段组合，实现了对溶解度预测中化学与结构贡献的清晰分解，并通过预训练-微调策略提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溶解度预测模型将物理化学描述符与分子图信息融合为单一表示，导致无法区分预测结果是源于全局化学性质、分子结构还是两者共同作用，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 采用双分支加性深度学习框架：化学分支用多层感知机编码物理化学描述符，结构分支用图神经网络编码分子图拓扑，两分支输出在预测阶段通过加性模型（可选乘性交互）组合。在AqSolDB上预训练，在BigSolDB2上微调。用最佳线性投影、分子级嵌入摘要、原子级GNNExplainer掩码进行解释。

Result: 在两个数据集上取得了有竞争力的预测性能；预训练-微调策略显著提升精度并降低运行间变异；化学分支与熟悉的物理化学描述符对齐，结构分支捕获图拓扑和官能团模式。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持预测性能的同时，使化学信息和结构信息的不同作用更加透明，增强了模型可解释性，且预训练策略具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [195] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究通过IPIP-50问卷操控GPT-4o模拟的美国和中国裔美国人角色的问题顺序，发现LLM角色表达包含两个可分离的组成部分：聚合特征（大五人格分数）对框架变化稳健但随机化下降21%；几何特征（SPD流形）在框架错位时崩溃（下降42%），但在共享框架下恢复至84%，优于聚合特征（76%）。这表明角色几何不是内在属性，而是框架依赖的协调模式，编码了聚合无法捕捉的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM角色评估通常仅使用心理测量问卷的聚合分数，忽略了实例内相关结构（几何结构），但尚不清楚这种几何结构是角色的内在属性还是依赖于框架（如问题顺序）的产物。

Method: 从IPIP-50问卷响应构建实例内相关矩阵，在SPD流形上分析几何结构，通过操控GPT-4o模拟美国和中国裔美国人角色的问题顺序，比较聚合特征（大五分数）与几何特征（SPD流形）在不同框架条件下的表现。

Result: 聚合特征在随机化下下降21%但对框架变化稳健；几何特征在框架错位时崩溃（下降42%），但在共享框架下恢复至84%，高于聚合特征的76%。几何特征的崩溃-恢复模式表明其并非内在属性而是框架依赖的协调模式。

Conclusion: 确立了LLM角色的双重性质框架：框架依赖的几何特征与框架稳健的聚合特征并存。这要求框架感知的评估方法，并挑战了静态特质概念。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Bilinear control of age--space structured populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01347)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有可再生边界条件和内源性监测反馈的非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程的双线性最优控制问题，建立了闭环适定性和弗雷歇可微性，推导了约化与反馈修正的伴随方程，并给出了一阶最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有反馈机制的非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程的双线性最优控制问题，该问题中控制以混合输运-扩散方程的系数形式出现，同时状态生成的标量可观测量同时影响内部动力学和更新律，形成非线性闭环控制-状态映射。

Method: 采用特征线温和公式化方法替代标准Lions-Magenes论证；将反馈导数识别为低秩扰动项；在Volterra核框架下证明传递算子是拟幂零的，从而获得伴随算子的显式预解表示。

Result: 建立了闭环系统的适定性和弗雷歇可微性；推导了约化和反馈修正的伴随方程；在Volterra核情形下得到了伴随算子的显式预解表示；将切换函数分解为约化分量和反馈诱导分量。

Conclusion: 成功建立了具有反馈机制的非局部种群方程双线性最优控制的一阶最优性条件，为后续数值计算和实际应用（如种群管理、流行病控制等）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study constrained bilinear optimal control for nonlocal age--space structured population equations with renewal boundary conditions and endogenous surveillance feedback. The control acts as a coefficient in a mixed transport--diffusion equation, while a scalar observable generated by the state enters both the interior dynamics and the renewal law. This produces a nonlinear closed-loop control-to-state map and a feedback-dependent adjoint system. Using a characteristic mild formulation rather than a standard Lions--Magenes argument, we establish closed-loop well-posedness and Frechet differentiability. We then derive the reduced and feedback-corrected adjoint equations. The feedback derivative is identified as a low-rank perturbation $\ell_{\bar y,\bar u}(p)(t)χ(a,x)$; in the Volterra-kernel regime, the associated transfer operator is quasinilpotent, yielding an explicit resolvent representation of the adjoint. Finally, we prove first-order optimality conditions and decompose the switching function into reduced and feedback-induced components.

</details>


### [197] [Boundary-Aware Quantization: Finite-Scale Decision Geometry of Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01478)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了后训练量化（PTQ）对DNN决策边界的影响，提出了多种边界变化度量指标（如局部logit边缘半径、一阶边界位移、切片边界Jaccard距离等），并在多个基准（Digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10）上实验发现：8-bit量化可保持标签不变但边界已显著改变（边界Jaccard 0.428）；4-bit时精度仍达0.9733但边界Jaccard升至0.970；基于边界感知的早停策略可显著降低决策翻转率和边界Jaccard；校准集边界Jaccard可高相关（r=0.947-0.994）预测留出集边界Jaccard；引入边界间隙舍入项可在精度略有牺牲下改善边界保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化是部署深度神经网络的关键技术，但现有研究主要关注量化对模型精度的影响，忽略了量化对决策边界的改变。然而，决策边界的改变直接影响模型的可靠性和可解释性，尤其是对安全关键应用。本文旨在系统量化后训练量化（PTQ）引起的决策边界变化，并提出边界感知的量化策略。

Method: 提出多种量化诱导决策边界变化的度量指标：局部logit边缘半径、一阶边界位移、法向变化、切片边界Jaccard距离、网格预测变化、多类交界点计数、低边缘边界带翻转。在多个数据集（Digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10）上进行PTQ实验，并引入校准-测试早停策略和固定比特边界间隙舍入项来优化边界保持。

Result: Digits基准：8-bit量化保持所有测试标签但边界Jaccard仅0.428；4-bit精度0.9733，边界Jaccard升至0.970，中位局部边界偏移0.0290。校准-测试早停将Digits留出翻转率从0.0094降至0.0022，边界Jaccard从0.825降至0.524。CIFAR-10：6-bit PTQ-W精度损失0.0029，改变5.3%留出决策和24.5%低边缘边界带决策。边界感知早停选择8-bit翻转率0.0083、边界Jaccard 0.048，优于按精度选择的6-bit（翻转0.0367、边界Jaccard 0.184）。固定比特边界间隙舍入在4-bit将边界Jaccard从0.457降至0.435，边界带配对顺序翻转从0.3600降至0.3558。校准集边界Jaccard与留出集边界Jaccard相关系数达0.947-0.994。

Conclusion: 量化即使在不降低精度的条件下也会显著改变决策边界，标准精度指标不足以捕捉这种变化；边界感知的早停策略和边界间隙舍入项可以有效改善量化模型的边界保持；校准集边界Jaccard可作为留出集边界变化的可靠代理指标，为后续量化方案设计提供了新的评估维度。

Abstract: We measured quantization-induced decision-boundary changes using local logit-margin radii, first-order boundary displacement, normal variation, slice-boundary Jaccard distance, grid prediction changes, multiclass junction counts, and low-margin boundary-band flips. On the digits benchmark, 8-bit weight quantization preserved all test labels while producing boundary-mask Jaccard \(0.428\) on the PCA slice; at 4 bits, accuracy remained \(0.9733\), while boundary Jaccard rose to \(0.970\) and median local boundary shift reached \(0.0290\). Interpolation between adjacent quantization levels localized the visible reconfigurations at multiclass junctions, with 12, 34, and 17 triple-junction cells in the selected transitions. Calibration-to-test stopping reduced the digits held-out flip rate from \(0.0094\) to \(0.0022\) and boundary Jaccard from \(0.825\) to \(0.524\); the same stopping rule also reduced flips on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST. On official CIFAR-10 subsets, PTQ-W selected by accuracy gave 6-bit flip \(0.0367\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.184\), whereas boundary-aware stopping selected 8-bit flip \(0.0083\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.048\). On full CIFAR-10 with three seeds, 6-bit PTQ-W lost \(0.0029\) accuracy relative to float, changed \(5.3\%\) of held-out decisions, and changed \(24.5\%\) of low-margin boundary-band decisions. A fixed-bit boundary-gap rounding term changed the trade-off at 4 bits by reducing boundary Jaccard from \(0.457\) to \(0.435\) and boundary-band pair-order flip from \(0.3600\) to \(0.3558\), with an accuracy trade-off; the 3-bit stress test exposed the tuning limit of this surrogate. Calibration boundary Jaccard predicted held-out boundary Jaccard across PTQ-W and optimized rounding variants with \(r=0.947\)--\(0.994\).

</details>


### [198] [Admission and Assortment Optimization for Multi-size Automated Parcel Lockers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01489)
*Carlos Aníbal Suárez,Antoine Deza,Tal Raviv*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多尺寸自动包裹柜的容量设计与准入控制问题，提出马尔可夫决策过程模型，分析始终接受策略的最优性条件，并通过精确算法和局部搜索启发式方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动包裹柜面临多种尺寸包裹和储物格的不匹配问题——小包裹可用大格子但会阻塞未来更大包裹（其拒收成本更高），因此需要在准入控制和容量设计上进行优化。

Method: 将准入问题建模为有限状态无限时域平均成本马尔可夫决策过程，通过相对值迭代精确求解小规模实例；分析始终接受（AA）策略并给出其最优性的充分条件；对于储物柜组合问题，提出边界枚举精确算法和基于交换邻域的局部搜索启发式方法。

Result: 在二、三、四尺寸实验中，AA策略在快速取件场景下最优，在较长持有时间下接近最优，观察到的最优性差距可忽略不计。局部搜索在可精确验证的实例中找到认证最优解，并可作为更大系统的启发式方法扩展。

Conclusion: AA策略在多数实际场景下足够好，局部搜索是求解储物柜组合问题的有效方法，即使目标函数不满足离散凸性。

Abstract: We study admission control and capacity design for automated parcel lockers with multiple parcel and locker sizes. A smaller parcel can use a larger locker, but doing so may block a future larger parcel whose rejection is more costly. We formulate the admission problem as a finite-state, infinite-horizon average-cost Markov decision process and solve small instances exactly by relative value iteration. We analyze the always-accept (AA) policy, which admits every feasible parcel into the remaining compatible capacity, and give a sufficient condition for its optimality. Across two-, three-, and four-size experiments, AA is optimal in fast-pickup regimes and nearly optimal when holding times are longer; observed optimality gaps are negligible even when AA is not optimal. We then study the locker-assortment problem, which minimizes facility cost plus optimal expected rejection cost. We give an exact bound-and-enumerate algorithm for moderate-size instances. Although the objective is not discrete convex, exchange-neighborhood local search finds the certified optimum in every instance for which exact certification is computationally tractable, and it scales as a heuristic to larger systems.

</details>


### [199] [Flexible and Reliable Network Design for Emerging Transportation Services: Multi-stage Stochastic Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01524)
*Riki Kawase,Koki Satsukawa,Toru Seo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个灵活且可靠的网络设计问题（FR-NDPs）通用框架，将其建模为风险厌恶的多阶段随机规划问题，并通过随机对偶动态规划求解。在曼哈顿中城网络的数值实验表明，灵活性与可靠性互补，风险厌恶型规划者会减少早期投资以保持适应性，并倾向于回避运营灵活性较低的交通模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享自动驾驶汽车等新兴交通服务中，历史数据稀缺且技术发展具有高度不确定性，现有网络设计方法难以同时兼顾灵活性和可靠性，亟需一个能够整合战略投资决策与运营控制的一般性框架。

Method: 将FR-NDPs建模为风险厌恶的多阶段随机规划问题，采用随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）求解，并建立了战略子问题与运营子问题收敛到全局最优的充分条件。

Result: (i)灵活性与可靠性互补，既能抵御极端场景又能减少期望性能损失；(ii)灵活性允许动态风险对冲，风险厌恶规划者减少早期投资以保留适应性；(iii)SAV与BRT系统运营灵活性的差异会反映在战略决策中，风险厌恶规划者倾向于不投资运营灵活性较低的交通模式。

Conclusion: FR-NDPs框架能有效应对新兴交通系统的高度不确定性，揭示了灵活性与可靠性的互补关系，以及运营灵活性对战略投资决策的重要影响。

Abstract: This paper proposes a general framework for flexible and reliable network design problems (FR-NDPs). The framework enables planners to change infrastructure investments in response to realized uncertainties, while ensuring desired levels of reliability. Motivated by emerging transportation services such as shared autonomous vehicle (SAV) systems, where historical data are scarce and technological developments uncertain, FR-NDPs integrate strategic investment decisions with operational control. We formulate the FR-NDPs as risk-averse multi-stage stochastic problems to be solvable by stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP) and establish sufficient conditions under which strategic and operational subproblems converge to the global optimum. We illustrate applications to SAV capacity expansion and integrated SAV-BRT (Bus Rapid Transit) route design, and numerical experiments on a Midtown Manhattan network highlight three key findings: (i) flexibility and reliability act complementarily to hedge against severe scenarios while mitigating the loss of expected performance; (ii) flexibility in investment planning allows dynamic risk hedging, with risk-averse planners reducing early-stage investments to preserve adaptability; and (iii) differences in operational flexibility between SAV and BRT systems are reflected in strategic decisions, with risk-averse planners tending to refrain investment in transport modes with lower operational flexibility.

</details>


### [200] [Mean Field Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01525)
*René Carmona,Mathieu Laurière*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从马尔可夫决策过程视角，系统介绍了平均场强化学习理论，包括与多智能体强化学习的联系、概率与控制论框架、代表性智能体学习问题、有限种群关系、动态规划原理、混沌传播极限、表格Q学习和策略梯度方法的理论分析，以及数值实现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立平均场控制理论与强化学习方法之间的连贯桥梁，为大规模随机种群设计可处理的学习方法，强调问题的数学结构。

Method: 从多智能体强化学习与平均场控制的联系出发，构建概率、数学和控制论框架，涵盖动态规划原理、混沌传播极限、表格Q学习和策略梯度方法的理论分析，以及表格方案和深度确定性策略梯度等数值实现。

Result: 形成了平均场强化学习的系统性理论框架，涵盖了一般模型和线性二次型模型的分析，并提供了理论分析结果与数值实现方法。

Conclusion: 该专著为平均场控制理论与强化学习之间提供了连贯的桥梁，强调了问题的数学结构以及大规模随机种群中可处理学习方法的设计。

Abstract: This monograph provides an introduction to mean field reinforcement learning through the lens of Markov decision processes arising from large-population stochastic control with mean field interactions and common noise. Starting from the connection between multi-agent reinforcement learning and mean field control, it develops the probabilistic, mathematical, and control-theoretic framework needed to formulate representative-agent learning problems, analyze their relationship with finite-population systems, and study both general and linear-quadratic models. The presentation includes dynamic programming principles, propagation-of-chaos limits, and theoretical analyses of tabular Q-learning and policy-gradient methods. It also discusses numerical implementations, including tabular schemes and deep reinforcement learning methods such as deep deterministic policy gradient. The goal is to give readers a coherent bridge between mean field control theory and reinforcement learning methodology, emphasizing the mathematical structure of the problems and the design of tractable learning approaches for large stochastic populations.

</details>


### [201] [Symbolic Discovery of Iterative Algorithms: A Continuous Latent Space Bayesian Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01552)
*Tongjia Liu,Ilias Mitrai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用潜空间贝叶斯优化的自动化迭代优化算法发现框架，通过变分自编码器将离散的更新函数空间映射为连续表示，进而进行高效搜索，在符号形式下发现了新的更新函数，且计算时间优于传统数学规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代优化算法依赖人工设计和数学规划方法，发现新算法的效率低且受限于预设的函数形式假设，亟需一种自动化的算法发现方法来突破这些限制。

Method: 将算法发现建模为离散优化问题，使用变分自编码器学习离散更新函数空间的连续表示，然后在该连续潜空间中采用贝叶斯优化搜索新的更新函数。

Result: 在两个案例研究中，该方法能够以符号形式发现新的更新函数，且计算时间低于现有的数学规划方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化框架无需对更新函数形式作任何假设即可发现新的迭代优化算法，具有更高的搜索效率和更好的泛用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the automated discovery of iterative optimization algorithms. We formulate the algorithm discovery task as a discrete optimization problem and search for new update functions using latent space Bayesian Optimization. The proposed framework first learns a continuous representation of the discrete space of update functions using variational autoencoders, transforming the algorithm discovery task from a discrete to a continuous search problem. The continuous representation is subsequently used to search for new algorithms using Bayesian optimization. Application to two case studies shows that the proposed approach can discover new update functions in symbolic form without any assumptions on the functional form of the update function. Moreover, the computational time required to discover the new update functions is lower than existing mathematical programming-based approaches.

</details>


### [202] [Multi-Domain Matrix Framework for Human Resource Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01613)
*Daehan Lee,Jaemyung Ahn,Jaewoo Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多域矩阵（MDM）的中小企业人力资源决策支持框架，通过将成员、技能和项目构建为相互关联的域，提供定性与定量分析工具，帮助初创企业诊断HR状态并支持工作量重新分配、招聘和能力发展等决策。案例研究验证了该框架在识别工作量失衡、发现关键成员超负荷问题以及指导招聘决策方面的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业面临三个核心挑战：组织组件间复杂的相互依赖关系、缺乏系统化的HR决策分析工具、以及快速变化环境下的响应需求。现有HR管理方法难以同时满足这三方面要求。

Method: 基于多域矩阵（MDM）将初创企业HR管理形式化为多域结构建模问题，将成员、技能和项目作为互联域构建集成MDM，提供定性分析指南和定量评估指标用于HR诊断和决策支持。

Result: 案例研究表明该框架能够识别工作量不平衡，揭示关键成员不可持续的工作负荷，并为后续招聘决策提供依据；招聘新成员后仍可继续应用以跟踪组织多域结构变化。

Conclusion: 所提出的MDM框架为中小企业提供了一种可操作的、系统化的HR决策支持工具，能够适应快速变化的组织环境并支持持续的HR诊断。

Abstract: This paper presents an actionable human resource (HR) decision-support framework for small firms and startups based on a multi-domain matrix (MDM). The framework addresses three key challenges faced by small organizations: complex interdependencies among organizational components; the lack of systematic analytical tools for HR decision-making; and the need for rapid responses in fast-changing organizational environments. The proposed framework formulates startup human resource management as a multi-domain structural modeling problem, where members, skills, and projects are interconnected domains within an integrated MDM. Based on this representation, the framework provides qualitative analysis guidelines and quantitative metrics for diagnosing an organization's HR state and supporting personnel decisions on workload redistribution, hiring, and capability development. A case study of MDM-based HR decisions for an early-stage technology startup is conducted to demonstrate the framework's practical applicability. The application shows that the framework can identify workload imbalances, reveal a key member with an unsustainable workload, and inform a subsequent hiring decision. The framework can be further applied after the hiring of a new member to track changes in the organization's multi-domain structure and support continuous HR diagnosis.

</details>


### [203] [Decentralized Stochastic Subgradient-type Methods with Communication Compression for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01755)
*Siyuan Zhang,Nachuan Xiao,Xin Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑非凸的分布式优化问题，提出一个统一框架，将多种带有压缩通信的分布式随机次梯度方法纳入其中，包括无偏压缩和带误差补偿的收缩压缩。通过将共识误差和平均迭代轨迹关联到连续时间微分包含，建立了在目标函数缺乏Clarke正则性时的全局收敛性。基于该框架，进一步开发了多种基于压缩的方法，包括使用符号正则化和梯度跟踪动量的分布式随机次梯度方法。数值实验验证了理论结果，并展示了通信-准确度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化研究大多假设目标函数光滑或具有Clarke正则性，且通信压缩方法的收敛性分析缺乏统一视角。本文旨在为非光滑非凸场景下的压缩通信分布式优化提供一个通用理论框架，并在此基础上开发新方法。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将无偏压缩和带误差补偿的收缩压缩两类方法纳入其中；利用微分包含的连续时间轨迹分析共识误差和平均迭代；基于框架开发了使用符号正则化和梯度跟踪动量的分布式随机次梯度方法。

Result: 证明了框架下所有方法在目标函数非光滑且缺乏Clarke正则性时的全局收敛性；数值实验验证了理论结果，并揭示了通信-准确度的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为非光滑非凸分布式优化中的压缩通信方法提供了严谨的理论基础，所提出的新方法在通信效率与收敛精度之间实现了有效的平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the nonsmooth nonconvex decentralized optimization problem, where inter-agent communication is compressed. We propose a general framework that unifies various decentralized stochastic subgradient-type methods with unbiased compression and contractive compression with error compensation. By relating the consensus-error iterates and the averaged iterates to the trajectories of continuous-time differential inclusions, we establish global convergence for all methods encompassed by our framework when the objective functions are nonsmooth and lack Clarke regularity. Based on our framework, we further develop several compression-based methods, including decentralized stochastic subgradient methods utilizing sign-based regularization and gradient-tracking momentum. Preliminary numerical experiments empirically support our theoretical results and highlight the communication-accuracy trade-off of the newly developed methods.

</details>


### [204] [Cognitive Warfare, Hybrid Pressure, and Sovereign Resilience: An Operations Research Framework Applied to the Iranian Case (2017--2026)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01894)
*Rahimeh Neamatian Monemi,Shahin Gelareh,Nelson Maculan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个耦合动力系统模型，将 grievance（不满情绪）和制度韧性作为连续过程，与优化马尔可夫决策过程选择的压力强度相结合，证明了均衡的存在性与局部稳定性，并通过伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）和30个随机网络实例验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运筹学中的攻防模型将防御方状态视为离散分配，而非连续、缓慢变化的制度过程，无法有效应对持续优化认知战压力的对手。

Method: 构建不满情绪与制度韧性的连续动力系统，耦合优化马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）选择压力强度；证明均衡存在性与局部稳定性；通过30个随机网络实例、全价值迭代和伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）进行验证。

Result: 伊朗案例位于计算的操作崩溃边界约25倍以上；贪婪播种策略在随机网络中平均网络渗透率达87%，显著高于度中心性基线。

Conclusion: 从业者可通过均衡与边界计算评估具体案例相对于崩溃边界的位置，而非依赖未经验证的压力强度对比。

Abstract: A defending state facing sustained economic, media, and psychological pressure from an adversary that continuously re-optimises its campaign poses a problem that existing attacker-defender models in operations research do not directly resolve, because they treat the defender's state as a discrete allocation rather than a continuous, slow-moving institutional process. We formulate a coupled dynamical system in which grievance and institutional resilience evolve continuously while pressure intensity is chosen by an optimising Markov decision process, prove existence and local stability of the resulting equilibrium, and prove a formal result distinguishing it from standard feedback-stability analysis and from a stationary Markov chain treated in isolation. We validate the framework computationally using thirty randomised network instances, full value iteration, and a documented case study of cognitive warfare directed at Iran (2017--2026). The historically calibrated case sits approximately twenty-five times above the computed operational collapse boundary, and a greedy seeding policy reaches eighty-seven percent average network penetration across the randomised instances, significantly above a degree-centrality baseline. A practitioner can use the equilibrium and boundary computation to assess where a specific case sits relative to collapse, rather than relying on an unverified comparison between opposing pressure intensities.

</details>


### [205] [Lower Bounds for Anytime Acceleration of Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02053)
*Chung-En Tsai,Ilyas Fatkhullin,Liang Zhang,Niao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降法在平滑凸优化中的随时收敛下界，证明没有任何正步长调度可以实现函数值最小化的 o(n^{-1.334}) 随时收敛率，以及平方梯度范数最小化的 o(n^{-1}) 随时收敛率。通过对二次函数和 Huber 函数变体的分析，推导出大步长数量和幅度的新上界，为 COLT 2024 开放问题提供了首个下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示，在已知总迭代次数 n 的情况下，梯度下降法通过使用可能违反下降性质的大步长可实现 O(n^{-1.271}) 的收敛率，但在未知 n 的随时收敛场景中，已知最优上界仅为 O(n^{-1.119})（函数值）和 O(n^{-1})（平方梯度范数），远低于经典的一阶方法下界 Ω(n^{-2})。这些差距表明当前上界是否可改进是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 本文通过分析梯度下降法在二次函数和 Huber 函数变体上的行为，推导出关于大步长数量和幅度的新上界，从而建立随时收敛的下界。具体而言，证明不存在任何正步长调度能在函数值最小化中达到 o(n^{-1.334}) 的随时收敛率，在平方梯度范数最小化中达到 o(n^{-1}) 的随时收敛率。

Result: 建立了两个随时收敛下界：(1) 函数值最小化的任意正步长调度无法达到 o(n^{-1.334}) 的随时收敛率；(2) 平方梯度范数最小化的任意正步长调度无法达到 o(n^{-1}) 的随时收敛率。这些结果为 Kornowski 和 Shamir 在 COLT 2024 提出的开放问题提供了首个下界。

Conclusion: 本文为梯度下降法的随时收敛率建立了下界，表明函数值最小化的最优随时收敛率介于 Ω(n^{-1.334}) 和 O(n^{-1.119}) 之间，平方梯度范数最小化的最优随时收敛率介于 Ω(n^{-1}) 和 O(n^{-1}) 之间（即紧界）。同时为 COLT 2024 开放问题提供了首个下界答案。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that the convergence rate of gradient descent (GD) in smooth convex optimization can be significantly improved by employing large stepsizes that may violate the descent property. In particular, if the total number of iterations $n$ is given, an $O(n^{-1.271})$ convergence rate can be achieved for both function value and squared gradient norm minimization. On the other hand, in the setting of anytime convergence, where $n$ is not known in advance, the best known rates of GD are much slower: $O(n^{-1.119})$ for function value minimization and $O(n^{-1})$ for squared gradient norm minimization. It remains open whether any of these upper bounds can be improved, as they are far from the classical $Ω(n^{-2})$ lower bound for any first-order method.
  In this work, we establish two lower bounds on the anytime convergence of GD. We show that no positive stepsize schedule can achieve an $o(n^{-1.334})$ anytime rate for function value minimization, nor an $o(n^{-1})$ anytime rate for squared gradient norm minimization. The key ingredients of our analysis are novel upper bounds on the number and the magnitude of large stepsizes, derived by analyzing GD on quadratic functions and variants of Huber functions. Our work provides the first lower bounds for the COLT 2024 open problem posed by Kornowski and Shamir regarding the optimal anytime convergence rates of GD.

</details>


### [206] [Invariance Entropy in the Dust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02279)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过单一构造对Kawan关于控制系统不变熵的两个自然推广问题给出否定回答，证明了有限严格不变熵不必等于普通不变熵，且严格不变熵在初始集的Hausdorff扰动下不必下半连续。该构造利用Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令、指数收缩坐标使后期不匹配几何上不可见、以及紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致，揭示了并非由动力扩张产生、而是由薄不变几何下精确可行性约束的持久性和不变熵中的极限顺序所驱动的信息复杂性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Kawan关于控制系统不变熵的两个长期未解（开放超过十五年）的自然推广问题。

Method: 单一构造方法：连续时间控制系统，包含存储无限符号指令的Cantor坐标、使后期不匹配几何上不可见的指数收缩坐标、强制精确符号一致的紧致匹配图。

Result: 1. 有限严格不变熵不必等于普通不变熵；2. 严格不变熵在初始集的Hausdorff扰动下不必下半连续。

Conclusion: 识别出一种信息复杂性来源，它并非由动力扩张产生，而是由薄不变几何下精确可行性约束的持久性和不变熵中的极限顺序所致。

Abstract: We answer negatively two natural general forms of Kawan's questions on invariance entropy for control systems, open for more than fifteen years, by a single construction. We show that finite strict invariance entropy need not coincide with ordinary invariance entropy, and that strict invariance entropy need not be lower semicontinuous under Hausdorff perturbations of the initial set. The construction is a continuous-time control system in which a Cantor coordinate stores an infinite symbolic instruction, an exponentially contracting coordinate makes late mismatches geometrically invisible, and a compact matching graph forces exact symbolic agreement. It identifies a source of information complexity not generated by dynamical expansion, but by the persistence of exact viability constraints under thin invariant geometry and by the order of limits in invariance entropy.

</details>


### [207] [Sensitivity Analysis and Robust Optimal Control for Coupled Evolution Inclusions with State-Dependent Maximal Monotone Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02339)
*Jinsheng Du,Boris Mordukhovich,Shengda Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由半线性发展包含和状态依赖极大单调算子微分包含构成的强耦合非光滑系统，在解映射性质、Bolza型优化、多种参数不确定性控制策略以及数值实验四个方面做出了贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理强耦合非光滑动力系统（含状态依赖极大单调算子）在参数不确定条件下的优化与控制问题，现有理论在解映射的连续性、多种控制策略的存在性方面尚不完善。

Method: 首先建立参数化解映射的适定性、紧性和Painlevé-Kuratowski连续性；然后对Bolza型优化证明最优对存在性、值函数连续性和最优解映射上半连续性；接着研究固定参数最优控制、同时控制-参数设计、min-max鲁棒控制和Hurwicz型折衷控制并证明存在性；最后通过数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 建立了参数化解映射的关键性质；证明了Bolza型优化问题最优解的存在性及值函数的连续性；为四种参数不确定性控制策略提供了存在性定理；数值实验展示了所预测的敏感性和鲁棒性现象。

Conclusion: 为包含状态依赖极大单调算子的强耦合非光滑系统的优化与控制提供了完整的理论框架，涵盖了参数不确定性的多种场景，理论和数值结果一致。

Abstract: We consider a class of strongly coupled nonsmooth systems consisting of a semilinear evolution inclusion and a differential inclusion governed by state-dependent maximal monotone operators. Our main contributions are fourfold. First, we collect the well-posedness, compactness, and Painlevé--Kuratowski continuity properties of the parameterized solution map required for the subsequent optimization analysis. Second, for Bolza-type optimization over the solution set, we prove the existence of optimal pairs, establish continuity properties of the value function, and derive upper semicontinuity of the optimal-solution map. Third, we study fixed-parameter optimal control, simultaneous control-parameter design, min--max robust control, and Hurwicz-type compromise control under parameter uncertainty, and we establish existence results for each formulation. Fourth, we report numerical experiments for sweeping-type systems that illustrate the sensitivity and robustness phenomena predicted by the theory.

</details>
